  - **API under `/api/v1/feedback/tickets/`** — list (`?scope=mine|public|all` plus `status/category/author/search/date_from/date_to/page` filters), detail (replies filtered server-side by viewer with admin → author → public → 404 fall-through precedence), POST create (multipart, validates each attachment via magic-byte check), PATCH (admin-only, `status` writable but `is_public` rejected with 400), `reopen` action (author-only, when status ∈ resolved/rejected), `reply` action (admin-only multipart; mode='public' atomically flips `ticket.is_public=true`), `admin_unread_count` action (admin-only count of `status='new'`).
  - **Email** — `services/email.py` resolves recipients = active users with `role='admin'` ∪ `FEEDBACK_ADMIN_EMAIL` env var; whole body wrapped in try/except so SMTP failure never breaks a ticket POST; dispatched via `transaction.on_commit` so a DB rollback never sends a stale email. Console backend in dev; SMTP via env vars in prod (`EMAIL_HOST/PORT/USER/PASSWORD/USE_TLS`, `DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL`, `PLATFORM_URL` for the deep link). `PLATFORM_URL` blank → email omits the URL line rather than render a broken link.
  - **File validation** — magic-byte check on first 12 bytes (PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF), 5 MB / 5 files-per-parent server-side caps, allowed-extension whitelist, `os.path.basename` to neutralise path traversal in `original_filename`.
  - **Tests (20 new cases on MSSQL, 20/20 pass)** — visibility precedence (admin sees all incl. internal; author sees own + non-internal; peer can't list or retrieve a private ticket; public ticket visible via `?scope=public`); reply-mode='public' flips `ticket.is_public`; PATCH with `is_public=true` rejected with 400; reopen by non-author returns 404 (consistent with module's "404 not 403" philosophy); bad upload (oversize / wrong magic bytes) rejected; email function returns silently with no recipients.
  - **Frontend** — four lazy-loaded routes (`/feedback/submit`, `/feedback/my-tickets`, `/feedback/public`, `/admin/feedback`) plus a fixed-position `FeedbackFAB` floating-button rendered from `AppLayout` so it appears on every authenticated page with `submitted_from_path`/`user_agent` auto-captured. `SubmitFeedbackPage` is a single-page Form with a right-side instructions panel (collapses to accordion on mobile). `MyTicketsPage` is a ProTable with a thread Drawer + Reopen action. `PublicFeedPage` is a server-paginated card list. `AdminInboxPage` is a two-pane (35%/65%) layout with status/category/author/date/search filters, a `ReplyComposer` with a three-way Radio for mode, and internal notes visually distinct (yellow background + tag). Two-pane layout collapses to list+Drawer on mobile.
  - **`ScreenshotInput` component** — four input methods: Ant Upload.Dragger (file picker + drag-drop), `paste` listener (Ctrl+V) that accumulates all clipboard images in one event, and html2canvas-based "Capture this screen" button (dynamic `import('html2canvas')` so it stays out of the main bundle). 5 MB / 5-file / image-MIME client-side validation; diff-based `useEffect` revokes orphaned `URL.createObjectURL` blobs on every files-array change to prevent leaks.
  - **Admin gate strict to `role==='admin'`** — backend permission `IsFeedbackAdmin` and frontend `FeedbackAdminGate` both check the role string only. Deliberately bypasses the project's shared `is_superuser` shortcut for `/admin/feedback` and the sidebar inbox entry, so a Django superuser whose actual role is `export_manager` cannot reach the inbox. Sidebar badge unread-count query (`useFeedbackAdminUnreadCount`) polls every 60 s and is `enabled: user?.role === 'admin'`.
  - **i18n** — `feedback.*` namespace in tk + ru + en covering nav labels, page titles, category/status names, form labels & validation, multi-paragraph instructions panel content (Win+Shift+S, Cmd+Shift+4, phone Power+Volume Down), reply-mode labels & tooltips, and toast messages. `submitted_from_label` translated at display time using a `pathLabels.ts` helper, so a Russian admin sees Russian labels for tickets submitted by Turkmen users.
  - **Out of scope (deferred to v1.1)** — voting/likes, SLA timers, priority levels, auto-close, Telegram notifications, "complaint" category, user-side notifications (no toast/bell on reply — user must visit My Tickets), structured shipment linking, audit-log entries (the reply thread is the audit trail).

- **Phase 5c of Sheet Control v2 — admin-created free-text custom rows** (feat(p3), L1 runtime per ADR-0004). Admins can now add rows to the Sheet from the Admin → Sheet Rows tab without a code change. Each row stores per-shipment free text in a child table; permission gating reuses the existing Phase 1 lock / role-trigger / extra-user machinery.
  - **Backend** — `SheetRowSetting.is_custom: BooleanField(default=False)` (read-only on the serializer; flipped only by POST create). New child model `ShipmentCustomFieldValue(shipment FK, row FK, value_text, updated_at, updated_by)` with composite UQ on `(shipment, row)`. Migration `0007_custom_rows` is purely additive: AddField (`is_custom`) + CreateModel (`ShipmentCustomFieldValue`).
  - **POST `/admin/sheet-rows/` re-enabled for custom rows.** Server enforces `field_key` starts with `custom_` (regex `^custom_[a-z0-9_]{1,53}$`), uniqueness, and at least one non-empty label. Sets `is_custom=True`, `display_order = max(existing) + 1024`, `row_number = max(100, max_row_number + 1)` so custom rows land below the legacy Excel layout (1–44) without colliding. Auto-grants the creating admin a `SheetRowUserPermission` so they can edit immediately. Writes an AuditLog row.
  - **PATCH `/shipments/{id}/custom-fields/`** — body `{field_key, value}`. `update_or_create` on `(shipment, row)`. Reuses `can_edit_sheet_field(user, field_key)` so locks / role triggers / extra-user grants from Phase 1 still gate custom rows. Empty string is allowed (= "explicitly cleared by user"; updated_at/by track the clearing). Blocks PATCH on archived shipments. Writes an AuditLog row.
  - **`/sheet/` payload extended.** Each shipment dict gains `custom_fields: {field_key: value}` (one extra grouped query, no N+1). The `rows` array synthesizes IRowConfig-shaped entries for every visible `is_custom=True` SheetRowSetting (input_type='text', default_who_key='sheet.who.custom') alongside the DEFAULT_SHEET_ROWS-backed entries. Order resolution honors per-user `UserSheetRowPref.position` for custom rows the same way it does for default rows.
  - **DELETE bypasses the 30-day cooldown for custom rows.** The cooldown protects DEFAULT_SHEET_ROWS from accidental nukes; custom rows are admin-owned at runtime and admins can recall them immediately. Default-row delete behavior unchanged.
  - **Frontend** — `IShipmentSheetItem.custom_fields?: Record<string, string>` and `ISheetRowSetting.is_custom: boolean`. `SheetCell.getCellValue` branches on `field_key.startsWith('custom_')` and reads from `shipment.custom_fields`. `SheetCellEditor` has a new `customFieldMutation` that PATCHes to the dedicated endpoint when the field is custom; `currentValue` resolution also branches. The Sheet Rows admin tab gains an **Add custom row** primary button + Modal with: slug input (auto-prefixed `custom_`, regex-validated client + server side) and three-language label inputs. Custom rows render with a small purple "custom" tag in the field_key column so admins can spot them.
  - **i18n** — `sheet_rows.add_custom_row`, `add_custom_hint`, `custom_badge`, `custom_modal_title`, `custom_modal_ok`, plus 8 form/error keys; `sheet.who.custom` fallback for the "Who" column when no per-row override is set. All in tk + ru + en parity.
  - **Tests (12 new in tests_custom_rows.py — 76/76 sheet-related tests pass on MSSQL)** — POST creates a custom row + auto-grants the creator; rejects non-`custom_` prefix, invalid characters, empty labels, duplicate field_keys; PATCH `update_or_creates` and is idempotent; empty-string PATCH persists as "cleared"; rejects unknown / non-custom field_keys; `/sheet/` payload includes `custom_fields`; DELETE skips the 30-day cooldown for custom rows.
  - **Out of scope** — typed custom rows (numeric, date, dropdown). Free text only; admins who need typed fields go through the L2 runbook (Phase 5b). Cross-shipment validation, formulas, and bulk import of custom values are deferred.

- **Phase 5b of Sheet Control v2 — runbook for adding typed Sheet fields** (docs(p3), L2 path per ADR-0004). New file `docs/runbooks/add_sheet_row.md` formalizes the existing developer process for adding a new column to the Shipment model + Sheet view. Covers: pick the right Django field type with the project's MSSQL gotchas (DecimalField for money, `**cyrillic_collation()` spread, string FK refs across apps, `db_column` for serializer-rename fields), `makemigrations`, `_ALL_PATCHABLE_FIELDS`, `DEFAULT_SHEET_ROWS`, `SheetCell.getCellValue` switch arm, `SheetCellEditor` render arm, `IShipmentSheetItem` + `IShipmentDetail` types, i18n in all three locales, and the auto-provisioner that creates the `SheetRowSetting` row idempotently. Includes a "Common mistakes" section calling out the project's recurring pitfalls.

- **Phase 5a of Sheet Control v2 — per-row "Who" column override** (feat(p3)). Admins can now override Col B (the "Who" / responsible-actor label) per row in three languages, the same way they already override Col C (the field label). `SheetRowSetting` gains `who_tk / who_ru / who_en` CharFields with `Cyrillic_General_CI_AS` collation. Migration `0006_sheet_row_setting_who_override` is a pure AddField triple. SheetLabelColumn resolves `setting?.who?.[currentUserLang]` first and falls back to `t(rowConfig.default_who_key)`. SheetRowsTab gains a third "Who (TK / RU / EN)" inline-input column between Label and Tooltip; reuses the existing `debouncedSave`. `/sheet/` payload's `row_settings[fk]` gains a `who: {tk?, ru?, en?}` block (null when all three are blank). 3 new tests pass on MSSQL.

- **Phase 4b of Sheet Control v2 — SLA escalation cron for stuck shipments** (feat(p3), ADR-0005). Hourly mgmt command (`python manage.py notify_stuck_shipments`) posts per-shipment Notification rows to active admin / director / boss users when a stuck shipment crosses the 8 / 15 / 30 day thresholds. Re-runs are no-ops once notifications exist; crossing the next threshold creates a new notification under a new kind.
  - **Notification model** — three new `KIND_CHOICES`: `stuck_8d` (entered the orange tier), `stuck_15d` (entered red), `stuck_30d` (extended critical). Migration `0005_notification_stuck_kinds` is a single AlterField on `kind.choices`. Frontend NotificationKind union and any icon mapping should pick these up automatically since the field is choices-based; no additional schema work needed.
  - **`notify_stuck_shipments` mgmt command** — one query for the candidate stuck shipments (≥ lowest threshold = 8 d, `is_archived=False`, `phase != COMPLETE`), one query for the recipient set (active users with role in `admin / director / boss`), one query for existing `(user_id, kind, link)` tuples to dedupe against, then one `bulk_create(batch_size=500)` for the missing notifications. Idempotent. Args: `--dry-run` (lists up to 20 candidates without writing). The `link` is `/shipments/<id>` so the frontend's existing notification renderer routes the user straight to the detail page.
  - **Recipient policy** — mirrors `ShipmentViewSet._STUCK_VIEW_ROLES` (`admin / director / boss`); the dashboard and the cron stay in sync via the same role list. `warehouse_chief` and `export_manager` are NOT recipients (the dashboard view is also empty for them).
  - **Cron entry — hourly** (separate from the daily archive cron because threshold crossings should surface within ~1h, not next-day). Documented in the command docstring (Linux + Windows). Registration is a deploy concern.
  - **Tests (9 new cases on MSSQL, archive+stuck+notify total 30/30 pass)** — 8 d threshold creates one notification per recipient (admin + director, NOT warehouse_chief); 18 d shipment generates both `stuck_8d` AND `stuck_15d` for the same user; 35 d shipment generates all three kinds; second run produces no duplicates; archived shipments and terminal-phase shipments are excluded; under-threshold (5 d) shipments are skipped; `--dry-run` writes nothing.
  - **Out of scope (future)** — SMS/Telegram/WhatsApp delivery of these notifications (master plan §3.7 explicitly defers to in-app only for now). Frontend NotificationKind icon styling for `stuck_*` is a tiny follow-up if the existing default icon is unsuitable.

- **Phase 4a of Sheet Control v2 — Stuck Shipments dashboard** (feat(p3), ADR-0005). Read-only management view of operational shipments untouched for 4+ days. The page lives at `/director/stuck-shipments`, role-gated to admin / director / boss. Notification escalation (Phase 4b) will pull from the same backend filter and is a separate commit.
  - **Backend (export/views.py)** — `ShipmentViewSet` gains a `?stuck=true` query mode and a `_STUCK_VIEW_ROLES = ('admin', 'director', 'boss')` constant. Filter: `is_archived=False AND status.phase != 'COMPLETE' AND updated_at <= now - 4 days`, ordered `updated_at` ascending so the worst case surfaces at the top. Threshold lives in `_STUCK_THRESHOLD_DAYS = 4` (matches the lower bound of the dashboard's color scale: 4–7 yellow / 8–14 orange / 15+ red). Non-management roles silently get an empty page (uniform with the archive role gate).
  - **Frontend page (pages/director/StuckShipments.tsx)** — ProTable view with shipment_code (linked to detail), status, days_stuck (color-coded tag), country, customer, last_touched. `daysSince()` helper computed in the browser from `updated_at` so we don't need a backend annotation. Summary card across the top with bucket counts (red / orange / yellow / total) for at-a-glance triage. Whole rows tinted by their bucket via `rowClassName`.
  - **Route + nav** — `App.tsx` lazy-loads `StuckShipments` behind `<ProtectedRoute roles={['admin', 'director', 'boss']}>`. Sidebar nav gains a **Main → Stuck Shipments** entry. The menu-item filter in `AppLayout.tsx` was extended to support an optional `roles` array per item — items with `roles` bypass `canSeePage()` and are gated by role inclusion instead. This is the first nav entry that's role-gated rather than `page_permissions`-gated; future role-only items reuse the same shape.
  - **i18n** — `nav.stuck_shipments` plus a `stuck.*` namespace with 16 keys covering page chrome, column titles, day-count plurals (`days_one` / `_other` for en/tk; `days_one` / `_few` / `_many` / `_other` for ru), bucket labels, and empty/error states. tk + ru + en parity verified.
  - **Tests (6 new cases on MSSQL, 21/21 archive+stuck pass)** — open + 5d → returned; recent (1d) → not returned; closed-and-stale → not returned (terminal phase excluded regardless of age); ordering oldest-first; archived rows excluded; admin allowed; warehouse_chief and export_manager get empty page (export_manager has shipment.view but stuck is tighter — director-level).
  - **Out of scope** — Phase 4b notification cron (when stuck count crosses thresholds, post a `Notification` to the director). Frontend bulk-action buttons ("Reassign", "Mark cancelled") were also deferred — current cut is read-only with click-to-detail; admins intervene from the detail page.

- **Phase 3 of Sheet Control v2 — Operational/Archive split + auto-archive cron** (feat(p3), ADR-0005). The shipments list now defaults to operational rows (`is_archived=False`); a new Archive segmented option (visible only to admin / director / export_manager / finansist / boss) opts into the read-only archive view. A daily mgmt command (`python manage.py archive_shipments`) flips terminal-phase shipments older than 21 days to `is_archived=True` — open shipments are never auto-archived, that's Phase 4's stuck dashboard's domain.
  - **Backend** — `Shipment.is_archived: BooleanField(default=False, db_index=True)` + `archived_at: DateTimeField(null=True)`. Migration `0004_shipment_archive_fields` is purely additive; defaults make every existing row operational without a backfill. `ShipmentViewSet.get_queryset` applies `is_archived=False` by default; `?archived=true` opts into archive view, gated to `_ARCHIVE_VIEW_ROLES`. `ShipmentViewSet.partial_update` returns 403 on archived rows even when `?archived=true` would otherwise let `get_object()` find them (defense in depth — frontend never sends this, but a crafted request would otherwise mutate). The `/sheet/` endpoint also filters `is_archived=False` — the Sheet has no archive equivalent.
  - **`archive_shipments` mgmt command** — selection: `status.phase='COMPLETE'` AND `is_archived=False` AND `updated_at <= now - <older_than> days` (default 21). Implemented as one `qs.update(is_archived=True, archived_at=now)`, bypassing `save()` so `updated_at` stays at the original last-touched timestamp; `archived_at` carries the archive event. Idempotent. Args: `--older-than N`, `--dry-run` (lists up to 20 candidates without writing). Cron entries documented in the module docstring (Linux + Windows Task Scheduler) — registration is a deploy concern.
  - **Frontend** — `useShipments.archived?: boolean` filter sends `?archived=true`. `ShipmentList.tsx` Segmented becomes three-way (`All / My Work / Archive`); the Archive option only renders for management roles via `ARCHIVE_VIEW_ROLES` (mirrors backend `_ARCHIVE_VIEW_ROLES` — keep both in sync). When `isArchiveView`: the action-column edit pencil renders null, row selection disabled (no inline edits, no bulk transitions), and the inline `weight_net` editable cell is also disabled. i18n: `shipments.archive` in tk/ru/en (parity verified).
  - **Tests (15 cases on MSSQL, all pass)** — endpoint filtering (default excludes archived; `?archived=true` returns archived only; admin/director/finansist allowed; warehouse_chief/sales_rep get empty page silently); the `partial_update` 403 guard with `?archived=true`; cron behaviour (terminal+stale → archived; terminal+recent → skipped; open+stale → skipped at any age including 90d; open+30d explicitly tested to pin the phase guard, not just the age guard; second run is no-op; `--dry-run` does not write; `--older-than` tightens the window).
  - **Out of scope** — Phase 4 (stuck shipments dashboard with SLA escalation for long-open rows) is a separate future commit. The duplicated role list (`_ARCHIVE_VIEW_ROLES` backend vs `ARCHIVE_VIEW_ROLES` frontend) is acknowledged tech debt — exposing the list via `/auth/me/` is the natural follow-up.

### Changed
- **Sheet freeze panes — moved from Toolbar dropdown to Sheet Display Settings modal** (feat(frontend)) — replaces the Google-Sheets-style "0 / 1 / 2 / Up to current row" dropdown with a `⚙ Settings` button (top-left toolbar) that opens a `Sheet Display Settings` modal. Modal contains two `Select` pickers: *Freeze rows up to* (one option per visible row, labelled `<field> (R<row_number>)`) and *Freeze columns up to* (`After column N`, capped at `min(20, shipmentCount − 1)`). Apply-on-change, no save button; `Reset to default` and `Done` in the footer. Badge dot on the gear button when freeze is non-default. Preserves the existing `sheetStore` state and `localStorage` persistence under `ygt-sheet-freeze` — only the configuration UI changed. The "Up to current row/column" shortcut is retired (picking from the modal is more discoverable and doesn't require pre-clicking a cell). 14 `sheet.freeze.*` i18n keys replaced with 12 `sheet.settings.*` keys in tk/ru/en.

### Added
- **`.env` configuration for backend and frontend** (chore) — `python-dotenv` added to `backend/requirements.txt`; `config/settings.py` now loads `backend/.env` on startup. Created `.env` and `.env.example` in `backend/` (Django + DB + CORS) and in `frontend/` (Vite `VITE_API_BASE_URL`, `VITE_USE_MOCK`). Both `.env` files are gitignored. **Removed hard-coded prod `DB_PASSWORD` default from `settings.py`** — non-DEBUG startup now raises `RuntimeError` if `DB_PASSWORD` is unset, surfacing missing config instead of silently using the production password. Also added `.vscode/settings.json` (gitignored) with `python.terminal.useEnvFile=true` and `python.envFile=${workspaceFolder}/backend/.env`.

### Changed
- **Collapsed MSSQL schemas to `dbo` with flat table-name prefixes** (refactor(db)) — eliminated three open mssql-django bugs that recurred whenever a model lived in a non-`dbo` schema: `_sql_select_default_constraint_name` (issue #402, open), M2M through-table double-bracket auto-naming (covered partially by an in-tree workaround migration), and `sql_flush` against schema-qualified tables (issue #496, open). All tables now live in `dbo`; `core_*`, `export_*`, `greenhouse_*` are name prefixes only.
  - **`schema_table()` returns `f'{schema}_{table}'`** in `apps/core/db_utils.py` (one-line change). The 52 callsites on model `Meta.db_table` now produce flat names like `core_export_firms`, `export_shipments`, `export_harvest_day_entries`. SQLite-vs-MSSQL split removed (both backends now return the same value). Critical invariant preserved: `HarvestDayEntry` (in the `greenhouse` Django app) keeps the `export_` prefix because the model owns the `export.harvest_day_entries` cross-app bridge — verified in the regenerated `apps/greenhouse/migrations/0001_initial.py`.
  - **Migration history nuked + regenerated.** All 56 pre-collapse migrations across `core` / `export` / `greenhouse` were `git mv`ed to `backend/_pre_collapse_backup/<app>/` for reference; `python manage.py makemigrations` regenerated three fresh `0001_initial.py` files containing only ORM `CreateModel` / `AddIndex` / `AddConstraint` ops — zero `RunSQL`, zero `cursor.execute`, zero `[schema].[table]` literals, zero `CREATE SCHEMA` statements. All `db_collation='Cyrillic_General_CI_AS'` declarations and all `Meta.constraints` (CHECK + UNIQUE) survive on the field/model level and were verified in the regen output. Dropped the `_is_sqlite()` helper in `db_utils.py`.
  - **6 data-seed migrations re-created** with explicit cross-app dependencies and `DJANGO_TESTING=true` guards (skip during tests so test `setUp` methods that create their own reference rows don't hit UNIQUE conflicts): `core/0002_seed_shipment_option_types`, `core/0003_seed_tomato_varieties`, `core/0004_seed_crate_types`, `core/0005_seed_greenhouse_config`, `core/0006_seed_shipment_draft_status` (moved from `export/` — model lives in core), `export/0002_seed_truck_split_defaults`. Pre-collapse data-*backfill* migrations (sheet display_order, harvest day-entry explosion, etc.) are intentionally not re-emitted because they operated on prior production state and the new DB cutover (step 7.5 of the migration plan) copies post-state directly from `YIGIT_PLATFROM`.
  - **`mssql_patches.py` deleted; custom `MSSQLSchemaTestRunner` deleted.** No more monkey-patching mssql-django's schema editor — the patched bug (`_sql_select_default_constraint_name` searching `sys.tables.name = '[core].[my_table]'`) is irrelevant for `dbo` tables. The custom test runner was incomplete schema-creation scaffolding that's no longer needed; Django's default `DiscoverRunner` handles `dbo`-only fine. `CoreConfig.ready()` no longer calls `apply_patch()`.
  - **`token_blacklist.0008_migrate_to_bigautofield` workaround** (`core/0007_pre_simplejwt_drop_blacklisted_token_unique`). simplejwt 5.3's `BlacklistedToken.token` is a `OneToOneField` → SQL Server creates an auto-named UNIQUE constraint on `token_id`. mssql-django 1.7's `_alter_field` drops the FK and the FK helper index but does *not* enumerate `sys.key_constraints`, so the auto-named UNIQUE survives and the subsequent `ALTER COLUMN token_id bigint NOT NULL` fails with `(4922) ALTER TABLE ALTER COLUMN failed because one or more objects access this column`. New migration uses `run_before = [('token_blacklist', '0008_migrate_to_bigautofield')]` to find and drop the auto-named UNIQUE constraint pre-`0008`. Fully scoped to that one column; documented removal criterion (delete when mssql-django merges a fix or simplejwt rewrites `0008`).
  - **New management command `dump_fk_map`** (`apps/core/management/commands/dump_fk_map.py`) — read-only introspection of `sys.foreign_keys` for the `core` / `export` / `greenhouse` schemas, emits `docs/MIGRATION_FK_MAP.md` with FK relationships + a topologically sorted table list. Used as input for the upcoming `migrate_data_to_flat_schema` data-copy command (step 7.5 of the migration plan). 49 tables / 93 FKs across the three legacy schemas, no cycles. Delete after the schema collapse is fully cut over.
  - **Verified end-to-end against a fresh `test_YIGIT_PLATFROM`.** Drop-and-recreate test DB, `python manage.py migrate` runs all migrations clean (no patches, no schemas, no `--keepdb`), `python manage.py test` discovers and runs all 351 tests — `token_blacklist.0008` passes, no schema-related crashes, no migration aborts. Targeted regression run on `apps.export.tests_shipment_field_audit` → 8 / 8 pass.
  - **Step 7 — `YIGIT_PLATFROM_NEW` provisioned.** Empty DB created on the same MSSQL instance (`10.10.11.233\YIGIT`); `DB_NAME=YIGIT_PLATFROM_NEW python manage.py migrate` ran clean (no patches, no schemas, no `--keepdb`). New `verify_new_db.py` script runs 8 INFORMATION_SCHEMA assertions: no legacy schemas, all 53 project tables in `dbo`, `HarvestDayEntry` correctly placed at `dbo.export_harvest_day_entries`, Cyrillic collations on sampled columns, all 6 sampled CHECK constraints present, no leftover patch tables, reference data seeded, all expected migrations recorded — 8/8 pass.
  - **Step 7.5 — data migration complete.** New `migrate_data_to_flat_schema` management command (`apps/core/management/commands/migrate_data_to_flat_schema.py`): runs against `YIGIT_PLATFROM_NEW`, references legacy via three-part naming (`YIGIT_PLATFROM.<schema>.<table>`) — no separate Django DB alias needed. Hard-coded mapping of 50 tables (1 dbo + 22 core + 27 export) in topological order from `dump_fk_map`, copies via `INSERT INTO dbo.<flat> (cols) SELECT cols FROM YIGIT_PLATFROM.<schema>.<table>` wrapped in `SET IDENTITY_INSERT ON/OFF` and a single transaction per table. Flags: `--dry-run`, `--allow-overwrite` (DELETE FROM new before INSERT — required when seeded reference rows are present), `--only <tables>`, `--csv <path>`. Emits `docs/MIGRATION_ROW_COUNTS.csv` (per-table legacy/new counts + delta). End-to-end run against production legacy: 50/50 tables copied, 4,896 rows, all deltas zero. Topo-sort bug in `dump_fk_map` (external references to non-pending tables blocking the BFS) fixed at the same time.
  - **Pre-existing test failures inventoried** in `docs/PRE_EXISTING_TEST_FAILURES.md` — 71 of 351 tests fail with patterns that were broken on `main` before this branch (missing `seed_permissions` calls, stale `monday_plan_kg` references on `WeeklyHarvestPlan`, `_make_shipment` not supplying a `status_id`, `test_config_api` URLs unrouted). Grouped by root cause for follow-up cleanup PRs. None are caused by the schema-collapse refactor.

### Added
- **Sheet Control v2 — Phase 2a backend: per-user row preferences** (feat(p3)) — users can now persist a personal row order and hide individual rows in the Shipment Sheet, independently of admin display_order and is_visible settings.
  - **New model `UserSheetRowPref`** (`export_user_sheet_row_pref`): one row per `(user, SheetRowSetting)` pair. `position INT NULL` (sparse, step 1024 — NULL inherits admin `display_order`); `is_hidden BOOL` (AND-composes with admin `is_visible`). UniqueConstraint `uq_user_sheet_row_pref`. No JSONField per ADR-0008. Migration `0035_user_sheet_row_pref` (depends on `0034`, additive only, no backfill).
  - **New endpoints** `GET/PATCH /api/v1/export/user/sheet-preferences/` — read or bulk-update current user's row order + hidden rows. PATCH is partial (absent key = no-op). Fully replaces each dimension when present (e.g. `hidden_rows: []` unhides all). Validates all submitted row_ids against `SheetRowSetting.objects.active()`; unknown/soft-deleted ids return 400 `{error: "unknown_row_ids", ids: [...]}`. Wrapped in `transaction.atomic()`. Uses `update_or_create` per row (≤50 rows — documented trade-off vs bulk_create). `IsAuthenticated` only; all querysets hard-scoped to `request.user`.
  - **`/sheet/` order resolution updated** (master plan §3.6 / ADR-0003): loads `UserSheetRowPref` in 1 extra query (never N+1); computes `effective_order = user.position ?? admin.display_order` and `effective_visible = admin.is_visible AND NOT user.is_hidden`; rows and row_settings are now served in effective_order (previously `DEFAULT_SHEET_ROWS` insertion order). New `user_preferences: {row_order: [...], hidden_rows: [...]}` top-level payload key so frontend knows the user's current state without a second API call.
  - **Tests** (`tests_user_preferences.py`, 18 tests, all green): GET empty, PATCH order (positions 1024/2048/3072), PATCH hide/unhide, combined PATCH, unknown row_id validation, soft-deleted row_id validation, user isolation, `/sheet/` payload honors user order and hide, admin is_visible=False stays hidden regardless of user prefs, query-budget assertion (≤20 total, adding 3 prefs ≤ baseline+1).
- **Sheet Control v2 — Phase 1 foundation** (feat(p3)) — full rewrite of the Shipment Sheet admin tab and the per-row permission model. Replaces the v1 single-`triggered_role` XOR model with multi-role triggers, multi-user grants, an explicit lock toggle, multi-language labels and tooltips, per-row style, soft-delete, and optimistic locking. Drops the "inactive triggered_user = lock" hack.
  - **Three new ADRs locked the design.** `ADR-0008` (no JSONField on MSSQL — split columns or child tables), `ADR-0009` (additive migrations, do not delete `0026/0028`), `ADR-0010` (explicit `is_locked` field; data-migration converts inactive-user-as-lock rows). Builds on existing ADRs 0001/0002/0006/0007.
  - **Models.** `SheetRowSetting` extended with `display_order` (sparse, step 1024), `is_visible`, `is_locked`, `label_tk/_ru/_en` and `description_tk/_ru/_en` (CharField with `Cyrillic_General_CI_AS`), `style_width/_align/_color`, `version` (auto-bumped in `save()`), `deleted_at` / `deleted_by`. New `SheetRowSettingManager` exposes `.active()` and `.visible()`. New child models `SheetRowRoleTrigger(row, role)` (replaces single `triggered_role`) and `SheetRowUserPermission(row, user, can_edit, deleted_at, ...)` with partial UQ `(row, user) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL` (MSSQL filtered index).
  - **Migrations.** Three additive migrations on top of the existing v1 schema. `0031_sheet_control_v2_base` adds the new columns, alters `triggered_user` semantics, runs two data steps (`backfill_display_order = row_number * 1024`, `migrate_inactive_user_locks` — sets `is_locked=True, triggered_user=None` for rows whose triggered_user was inactive). `0032_sheet_row_role_trigger` creates the role-trigger child table, copies existing `triggered_role` values into it, drops the old XOR check constraint, drops the column. `0033_sheet_row_user_permission` creates the user-permission child table. All `bulk_create`/`bulk_update` carry `batch_size=500`.
  - **Permissions rewrite.** `apps/core/permissions.py::can_edit_sheet_field` and `get_sheet_edit_map` rewritten for ADR-0001 + ADR-0010 semantics: superuser/admin/director immune; hidden rows blocked; `is_locked=True` requires user match OR role-trigger match OR active extra-user grant (each AND'd with field perm); soft-deleted user grants ignored. Query budget for `get_sheet_edit_map` is now ≤4 with `prefetch_related('role_triggers', 'user_permissions')` (was ≤2 in v1) — the threshold is documented in the docstring and the test asserts the new bound.
  - **Admin endpoints (URL switched from `field_key` to numeric `id`).** `GET/PATCH /admin/sheet-rows/{id}/` (PATCH requires `version` body field, returns 409 with `current_version` on mismatch — version is mandatory, not optional, per ADR-0006). `POST /admin/sheet-rows/reorder/` body `{order: [id, ...]}` updates a single row's `display_order` to the average of its neighbours; rebalances all rows when gap < 2 (ADR-0007). `POST /admin/sheet-rows/permissions/bulk/` body `{row_id, grants[], revokes[]}` is idempotent (revokes set `deleted_at`; grants `update_or_create` with `deleted_at=None`). `DELETE /admin/sheet-rows/{id}/` soft-deletes and rejects with 400 unless `is_visible=False` for ≥30 days. `POST /admin/sheet-rows/{id}/restore/` clears `deleted_at`. `?include_deleted=1` shows soft-deleted rows. AuditLog continues to record per-changed-field rows; reorder writes a single summary AuditLog entry.
  - **`/sheet/` payload — Sheet Control v2 compact format.** New top-level keys: `users_index` (Record<user_id, {name, role}>), `current_user_id`, `current_user_lang`. `row_settings[field_key]` now carries `is_locked`, `is_visible`, nested `labels {tk,ru,en}`, `description {tk,ru,en}`, `style {width?, align?, color?}`, `triggered_user_id`, `triggered_roles[]`, `extra_user_ids[]`, `can_current_user_edit`, `version`, `settings_updated_at`, `settings_updated_by_id`. Hidden rows are filtered from BOTH `rows` and `row_settings` so the frontend never iterates them.
  - **Frontend admin tab rewrite.** `SheetRowsTab.tsx` rewritten (~545 lines) with inline-edit columns for labels (3 langs), tooltip popover for descriptions, visibility/lock `Switch`es, style popover (width slider 50–500, align radio, color picker), multi-role `Select`, multi-user `Select` wired through `useBulkPermissions` (diffs old vs new). 409 conflict triggers a `Modal.confirm` with a single Refresh action. Drag-reorder posts to the new sparse-update endpoint with optimistic cache. Tier 1/2/3 plate above the table (master plan §5).
  - **Frontend Quick View.** `ShipmentSheet.tsx` reads `row_settings[fk]` from the new payload. Lock icon (`LockOutlined`) renders next to row label when `is_locked=true && can_current_user_edit=false`. Labels prefer `row_settings[fk].labels[current_user_lang]`, falling back to the existing i18n `t(label_key)` mechanism. `style.width / align / color` applied to cells when defined. **Per-row `style.width` in this column-major layout is a known visual mismatch** (column header stays at `COL_WIDTH_SHIPMENT`); admins should leave it unset for now — flagged as Sprint 2 cleanup along with line-count split of `SheetRowsTab.tsx` and the systemic `IRowConfig` snake_case vs `sheetRowConfig.ts` camelCase pre-existing TS debt.
  - **Hooks.** New / rewritten: `useSheetRowSettings`, `useSaveSheetRowSetting` (typed 409 error, refresh modal), `useReorderSheetRows`, `useBulkPermissions`, `useSoftDeleteSheetRow`, `useRestoreSheetRow`. All keyed by numeric `id`, all use the existing httpOnly-cookie axios instance.
  - **Mock data.** `mock/shipmentSheet.ts` updated to the v2 payload (locked row, role-triggered row, extra-user-granted row).
  - **i18n.** New keys in tk / ru / en for the rewritten admin tab columns, popovers, modals, lock tooltip, and the Tier 1/2/3 plate.
  - **Out of scope (deferred).** Per-user row order + IndexedDB cache + BroadcastChannel multi-tab sync (Phase 2). `Shipment.is_archived` + auto-archive cron + Archive view (Phase 3). Stuck shipments dashboard with SLA escalation (Phase 4).
  - **Tests.** Rewritten / extended: `tests_sheet_perms.py` (lock + extra-user matrix, multi-role match, soft-deleted grants ignored, query-count regression guard at ≤4), `tests_sheet_settings_admin.py` (409 on stale version, mandatory-version 400, sparse reorder + rebalance, bulk grant/revoke idempotency, soft-delete pre-condition, restore round-trip, AuditLog assertions), `tests_shipment_sheet.py` (compact payload shape, `users_index`, hidden rows filtered from both `rows` and `row_settings`). Tests run under SQLite in CI; local MSSQL skipped due to missing `CREATE DATABASE` perm.
### Fixed
- **`tests_sheet_settings_admin.py` regressions from Phase 2a version-locking** (fix(p3)) — 7 PATCH tests written for Phase 1 started failing with `400 {'error': 'version is required for updates.'}` after the Phase 2a rewrite of `views_sheet_settings.py` made the `version` body field mandatory (was optional in Phase 1). All PATCH bodies now supply `'version': row['version']`; sequential double-PATCHes in the same test use the bumped version from the first response. (Confirmed Phase 2a regression via `git diff da40347 -- views_sheet_settings.py`.)
- **`tests_shipment_sheet.py` pre-existing test bugs** (fix(p3)) — three tests added in the `da40347` commit were silently broken before Phase 2a work began:
  - `test_firm_splits_auto_fill_official_kg` and `test_firm_splits_auto_fill_official_kg_three_firms` assumed `TruckSplitDefault` rows for `num_firms=2` (9,000 kg) and `num_firms=3` (6,000 kg) were seeded in `setUpTestData`, but no seed call existed. Without seed rows, `get_default_truck_weight(N)` falls back to `18100/N` (9,050 and 6,033.33), so both tests were returning the wrong expected values. Fixed by adding `TruckSplitDefault.objects.get_or_create()` + `invalidate_truck_split_cache()` in `setUpTestData`.
  - `test_block_sources_explicit_weight_honored` compared sorted string weights (`sorted(['12000.00', '6500.00'])`) lexicographically — string `'1'` < `'6'` so `'12000.00'` sorted before `'6500.00'`, reversing the expected order. Fixed by converting to floats before sorting.
  - (Confirmed pre-existing via `git worktree` checkout of `da40347` — all three fail identically at that commit with no Phase 2a involvement.)

### Added
- **Forecast layer for Weekly Harvest Plan** (feat(p3)) — daily-grain harvest data: each block-day cell now carries Plan, Forecast, and Actual values with full per-cell audit (timestamps, submitters, submission-window state, revision counts). KPI computation is deferred but every audit field needed to compute KPIs later is captured from day one. See **ADR-017**.
  - **`HarvestDayEntry` (daily grain) replaces 12 wide columns on `WeeklyHarvestPlan`.** New table at `export.harvest_day_entries` (one row per `weekly_plan × entry_date`) holding `plan_value` / `forecast_value` / `actual_value` plus per-layer audit columns (`*_submitted_at`, `*_submitted_by`, `plan_state`, `forecast_window`, `forecast_revision_count`, `actual_finalized_at`, `actual_source`) plus `last_override_at/by/reason` snapshot. Empty-vs-zero distinction: `value IS NULL` = "not entered" (em-dash), `value = 0 AND *_submitted_at IS NOT NULL` = explicit confirmed zero (italic). `weekday` allows 0–6 so end-of-season Sunday harvesting is supported (admin-entered, not bitmask-gated). Migration `greenhouse.0004_harvestdayentry_harvestdispatchlog_and_more` is atomic: creates new tables, backfills 6 daily rows per existing weekly plan (1,998 rows from 333 plans, preserving "never entered" via the zero-AND-no-submission rule → 257 NULL/1,741 valid plan_values), drops the wide columns + approval fields, drops FK constraints + indexes for the dropped FKs via `RunSQL` (`DF_whp_status` default constraint also dropped — works around the mssql-django default-constraint-drop bug), adds `locked_at`.
  - **Approval workflow REMOVED — submission is final** (per user direction). `WeeklyHarvestPlan.status` / `approved_at` / `approved_by` / `rejected_at` / `rejected_by` / `rejection_note` columns dropped. `submit_harvest_plan` / `approve_harvest_plan` / `reject_harvest_plan` services removed. `submit` / `approve` / `reject` / `bulk-submit` / `bulk-approve` / `bulk-reject` viewset actions removed. Replaced by `submit_weekly_plan(weekly_plan, user)` which records `submitted_at` / `submitted_by` and computes `plan_state` (`on_time` / `late` / `critical_late`) per HarvestDayEntry from current time vs config-driven deadlines.
  - **Tighter permissions, AD-15-aligned.** New role constants in `apps/core/roles.py`: `HARVEST_DAY_WRITE = {'admin', 'greenhouse_manager', 'warehouse_chief'}`, `HARVEST_DAY_OVERRIDE = {'admin'}` (admin-only with required `reason` text). `PLAN_WRITE` / `PLAN_APPROVE` removed (no callers after approval-flow drop). Service-layer enforcement: greenhouse_manager can edit Plan + Forecast (primary window) for their own assigned blocks; warehouse_chief can edit Forecast (fallback / same-day windows) and Actual on any block; admin can edit anything anytime with required `reason` that writes to `AuditLog` and `last_override_*` snapshot; export_manager / director are view-only.
  - **`GreenhouseConfig` singleton + `OperatingDayException`.** New `core.greenhouse_config` table (one row, `pk=1` enforced via `save()` override) for admin-tunable values: `plan_deadline_weekday` (default 4=Friday), `plan_late_until_weekday` (6=Sunday), `plan_critical_late_at_weekday` + `_at_time` (Monday 00:00), `forecast_primary_open` / `_close` (17:00/18:00 day-before), `forecast_fallback_close` (09:00 day-of), `forecast_same_day_close` (23:59), `notification_lead_minutes` (60), `truck_capacity_kg` (18500 — was hardcoded in frontend), `operating_days_bitmask` (0b0111111 = Mon–Sat by default; bits 0–6 = Mon–Sun), `timezone_name` (Asia/Ashgabat). `OperatingDayException(date UNIQUE, is_holiday, note)` for ad-hoc holiday calendar — dispatcher skips notifications/auto-tasks on `is_holiday=True` days.
  - **Time-based dispatcher (mgmt command + system cron, NOT Celery).** `apps/greenhouse/dispatcher.py` exposes pure `evaluate_triggers(now_local, config) -> list[TriggerEvent]` and `fire(event)` with idempotency via `HarvestDispatchLog(trigger_kind, target_user, scope_date)` UNIQUE. Six triggers: T1 forecast_nudge (forecast_primary_open − lead_minutes, to managers with missing forecasts for tomorrow), T2 forecast_handoff (forecast_primary_close, to warehouse_chief with gap list), T3 forecast_escalation (forecast_fallback_close, to warehouse_chief + admin + director, urgent), P1 plan_deadline_reminder (Friday morning to manager), P2 plan_late (Saturday morning), P3 plan_critical_late (Monday 00:00 to manager + admin). Run via `python manage.py run_harvest_dispatcher` from system cron every 5 min. **No SMS** in this iteration — in-app notifications only. **No Celery / Redis** — chosen mgmt-command + cron after comparing Celery+Redis / Django-Q2 / APScheduler (zero new deps, idempotent by DB UNIQUE, ≤90 trigger events/day doesn't justify a queue; revisit Celery when pallet rollup arrives). Cron entry documented in `docs/operations/cron.md` (Linux + Windows Task Scheduler).
  - **Personal kanban auto-tasks: TODO no-op.** Dispatcher includes `# TODO: evaluate_kanban_rules(event)` at the no-op call site. The five harvest auto-task rules (plan-submit nudge, plan-overdue, forecast-submit nudge, sklad fallback, escalated forecast) land in a follow-up commit when the parallel kanban work ships — avoids speculative coupling.
  - **`Notification.KIND_CHOICES` extended.** Six new kinds: `forecast_nudge`, `forecast_handoff`, `forecast_escalation`, `plan_deadline_reminder`, `plan_late`, `plan_critical_late`. Dropped: `plan_submitted` / `plan_approved` / `plan_rejected` (no callers after approval-flow removal). Migrations `export.0029_alter_notification_kind` + `0030_alter_notification_kind_stage2`.
  - **API surface.** New `GET/PATCH /api/v1/greenhouse/day-entries/`, `GET /api/v1/greenhouse/day-entries/{id}/history/` (returns `AuditLog` rows + override snapshot for the cell-history modal). PATCH body: `{"plan_value"|"forecast_value"|"actual_value": ..., "reason": "..."}` — service routes through `set_plan_value` / `set_forecast_value` / `set_actual_value` based on which field is in the body. Admin override required `reason` field that writes `AuditLog.detail = "OVERRIDE: {reason}"` + `last_override_*` snapshot. New `GET/PATCH /api/v1/core/greenhouse-config/` (admin only, returns the singleton). New `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /api/v1/core/operating-day-exceptions/` (admin writes, all read).
  - **Frontend.** `HarvestCell` component (`frontend/src/components/HarvestCell.tsx`) replaces dual `PlanCell` + `ActualCell` — context-determined display via cell date relative to today: future days show plan_value (blue), tomorrow during forecast primary window shows editable forecast input pre-filled with plan_value + grey "Plan: 12,000" hint underneath, tomorrow forecast-submitted shows yellow locked, today shows yellow forecast + editable actual input next to it, past days show actual (green) + click-to-history. Empty cells render em-dash for `value === null`, italic-with-checkmark for `value === 0 && *_submitted_at`. `WeeklyPlanGrid` rewritten (753→630 lines): uses `useDayEntries(week_start, week_end)` keyed by `${blockId}-${dateISO}`, header tile row replaced with **Total Plan / Total Forecast / Total Actual / Est. Trucks** where Est. Trucks divides "most-current value per cell" (forecast→plan→actual chain) by `truck_capacity_kg` from config (no longer hardcoded 18,500). Approve / reject / bulk-action UI dropped entirely. New `CellHistoryModal` opens on cell click — shows current values + audit log + admin overrides with reason text. New `AdminOverrideReasonModal` — admin edits open this modal first (required `reason` text, blocks save until non-empty). New `FallbackForecastView` page at `/greenhouse/fallback-forecast` for warehouse_chief — single-day vertical list of all blocks during fallback window (18:00 day-before – 09:00 day-of), batch-save button. `useGreenhouseConfig` / `useUpdateGreenhouseConfig` / `useOperatingDayExceptions` / `useDayEntries` / `useUpsertDayEntry` / `useDayEntryHistory` hooks. `useApproveHarvestPlan` / `useRejectHarvestPlan` / `useBulkSubmitHarvestPlans` / `useBulkApproveHarvestPlans` / `useBulkRejectHarvestPlans` removed. Wide-column fields removed from `IWeeklyHarvestPlan` interface. New types: `IHarvestDayEntry`, `IGreenhouseConfig`, `IOperatingDayException`, `IDayEntryHistoryItem`, `ForecastWindow`, `PlanState`, `ActualSource`. `MOCK_DAY_ENTRIES` added for VITE_USE_MOCK mode.
  - **i18n keys (tk/ru/en parity).** `plan.forecast`, `plan.total_forecast`, `plan.window_primary` / `_fallback` / `_same_day_red_flag`, `plan.state_on_time` / `_late` / `_critical_late`, `plan.cell_plan_hint`, `plan.empty_explicit_zero`, `plan.cell_history_title` / `_no_audit`, `plan.override_reason_label` / `_required` / `plan.override_modal_title`, `plan.fallback_mode_title` / `_help` / `plan.fallback_save_all` / `_already_submitted` / `_nothing_to_save`, `plan.toast_forecast_saved` / `_save_error`, `plan.audit_action` / `_field`. Dropped unused approval-workflow keys.
  - **Out of scope (deferred).** KPI computation and dashboards (data is captured), Auto-Actual via pallet rollup (manual entry remains), per-greenhouse override of deadline times, SMS / Telegram / WhatsApp channels, variety-level forecasting, auto-suggested forecasts, personal kanban auto-task wiring (lands in follow-up).
  - **Tests.** 29 new tests in `apps/greenhouse/tests/test_dispatcher.py` (all pure, no DB) covering `compute_plan_state` / `compute_forecast_window` boundaries, `_within_5min` / `_is_operating_day` / `_next_plan_week_start` helpers, T1/P3 trigger semantics, idempotency. 26 new tests in `apps/core/tests/test_config_api.py` covering GreenhouseConfig GET/PATCH (admin-only writes, validation of weekday/timezone/bitmask) and OperatingDayException CRUD (admin writes, all read, date-range filter). All pass on both SQLite (`USE_SQLITE=true`) and MSSQL (`--keepdb`).

- **`admin` role separated from `director` and `export_manager`** (feat(core)) — system-administrator capabilities now live on a single new `admin` role; `director` and `export_manager` keep all operational power but lose access to permission-matrix editing and user role/permissions management. See **AD-15**.
  - **`admin` role + `bootstrap_admin` management command.** `ROLE_CHOICES` gains `('admin', 'Admin')` (prepended). New idempotent `python manage.py bootstrap_admin` promotes every `is_superuser` user to `role='admin'` — replaces the previous `manage.py shell -c "..."` one-liner across CI / staging / onboarding / restore-from-backup workflows.
  - **Hardcoded gates flipped admin-only.** `_DirectorOnlyPermission` → `_AdminOnlyPermission` in `apps/core/views_permissions.py` (predicate: `is_superuser OR role=='admin'`) — covers all four matrix endpoints (page / resource / field / registry). `_DIRECTOR_ONLY` → `_ADMIN_ONLY` in `apps/export/views_admin.py` for `PATCH users/{id}/` (role/is_active) and `PUT users/{id}/permissions/`. Audit-log gate switched to a new `AUDIT_VIEWERS` constant (admin + director + EM).
  - **Reference-data writes EXPANDED, not flipped.** `apps/core/roles.py` exports `REFERENCE_DATA_WRITE = frozenset({'admin', 'director', 'export_manager'})`. `core/views.py` (Country, City, ShipmentStatusType, Customer, BorderPoint, TruckDestination, ShipmentOptionType) and `greenhouse/views_admin.py` (GreenhouseBlock, BlockManagerAssignment) now use this set — admin is added; director and EM keep current write access. Reference-data is operational, not administrative.
  - **Last-admin guard.** `UserManagementViewSet.partial_update` blocks demoting or deactivating the last active admin in the system (with 403 + explanatory message). Admin can promote freely; multiple admins are allowed.
  - **Seed defaults updated.** `admin` row gets every page (`_ALL_PAGES`), full CRUD on every resource (including `truck_split_default`), `'*'` field wildcards. `director` page set narrowed: `_ALL_PAGES - _ALL_ADMIN` (drops admin.*; analytics.boss survives — different prefix). `export_manager` loses the prior `admin.permissions` exception. Resource and field defaults for director/EM unchanged. New `admin.shipment_settings` entry added to `PAGE_REGISTRY` (it was referenced by `frontend/src/App.tsx` but missing from the registry — would have been blocked for everyone except superusers).
  - **Migrations.** `0015_add_admin_role` (AlterField on User.role + three permission tables to refresh the choices list with admin prepended). `0016_demote_existing_director` (idempotent data migration removing admin.* page rows from director and the admin.permissions leak from EM — without this, environments that previously ran `seed_permissions` would have the demotion be a no-op).
  - **Sheet-cell bypass narrowed.** `permissions.py` `can_edit_sheet_field` and `get_sheet_edit_map` extended to bypass for `admin` in addition to director — admin gets the same "fix anything" immunity to `SheetRowSetting` trigger gates as the operational lead.
  - **`IsBossOrDirector` includes admin.** Boss-dashboard analytics endpoints are now reachable by admin too. Class-rename to `IsBossDirectorOrAdmin` filed as a follow-up ticket (TODO comment in code).
  - **Frontend.** `UserRole` union prepends `'admin'`. `UsersPage` adds admin to `ALL_ROLES`, `ROLE_COLORS` (admin: red, director: volcano), and renames `isDirector` → `isAdmin` (matches backend gate). `roles.admin` label added to tk/ru/en (Admin / Администратор / Admin).
  - **Tests.** 30 new tests in `apps/core/tests_permission_matrix.py` (admin-only matrix gating + last-admin guard) and `apps/core/tests_reference_data_perms.py` (admin/director/EM all write reference-data; sales_rep blocked). 1 admin role-gating test added to `tests_boss_analytics.py` and `tests_truck_split_admin.py` each. All pass under `USE_SQLITE=true`.
- **Sheet configurable per-row trigger (role / user) + cell-level edit audit** (feat(p3)) — full delivery of plan D1–D8. Each Sheet row's "Who" column is now configurable in **Shipment Settings → Sheet Rows tab** as either a formal role or a specific user, and that selection acts as both label and edit gate. Every cell edit is recorded with structured `(field, old, new, user, timestamp)` and exposed inline in the Sheet via a hover marker plus click-to-expand history popover.
  - **Backend row map is the single source of truth (D1).** `backend/apps/export/sheet_rows.py` exports `DEFAULT_SHEET_ROWS: list[dict]` (42 entries, row 16 absent — mirrors the original Excel layout). The `/api/v1/export/shipments/sheet/` response now ships `rows` alongside the existing `results` / `comment_counts` / `task_counts`. Frontend `SHEET_ROW_CONFIG` constant deleted; `useShipmentSheet` reads `rows` from the API and the Sheet renders whatever the backend returns. New `frontend/src/constants/sheetOptions.ts` registry resolves `options_source` keys (e.g. `vehicle_condition`) to option arrays so dropdowns survive the API migration.
  - **`SheetRowSetting` model (D2 / D3).** `backend/apps/export/models/sheet_settings.py` — `field_key` unique (default Django `id` PK preserved so `AuditLog.object_id` stays compatible), `row_number` index, `triggered_role` (CharField with `ROLE_CHOICES`) XOR `triggered_user` (FK to `core.User`) enforced by `CheckConstraint`, `updated_at` / `updated_by` audit columns. Migration `export.0026_sheet_row_setting`.
  - **`AuditLog` extended for cell-level edits (D8 schema).** Migration `export.0027_auditlog_field_columns` adds `field_name` (CharField + index), `old_value` and `new_value` (both `TextField` + `Cyrillic_General_CI_AS`) plus composite `audit_field_history_idx (model_name, object_id, field_name, -created_at)`. Uses `SeparateDatabaseAndState + RunPython` raw SQL to dodge the mssql-django default-constraint-drop bug on schema-qualified tables; SQLite tests bypass migrations via `MIGRATION_MODULES`.
  - **Permission helpers (D4).** `backend/apps/core/permissions.py` — `can_edit_sheet_field(user, field_key)` composes `RoleFieldPermission` with the `SheetRowSetting` trigger gate (director / superuser bypass; inactive `triggered_user` locks the row for everyone). `get_sheet_edit_map(user, settings_by_key=None)` returns `{field_key: bool}` for every `DEFAULT_SHEET_ROWS` row in 2 queries (1 if `settings_by_key` already loaded by the caller).
  - **Cell-level edit audit write hook (D8).** `backend/apps/export/services/sheet_audit.py` — `render_field_value()` (single source of truth: `__str__` for FK objects so `"Begjan"` not `"5"`, `format(d, 'f')` for Decimals so no scientific notation, `.label` for TextChoices, `.isoformat()` for date/datetime), `snapshot_fields()`, `diff_audit_rows()`. `ShipmentViewSet.partial_update` now snapshots the submitted-keys subset before save, runs save + audit `bulk_create(batch_size=500)` inside one `transaction.atomic()`. Same-value PATCH writes zero rows. Existing 403/400 forbidden-field path preserved unchanged. Replaces the prior `logger.info(...)` line.
  - **`/sheet/` payload (D8 read path A).** Three new top-level keys: `rows` (D1), `row_settings` (`{field_key: {triggered_role, triggered_user, triggered_user_name, triggered_user_active, triggered_label, can_current_user_edit}}`), `last_edits` (sparse `{shipment_id_str: {field_key: {user_id, user_name, old_value, new_value, edited_at}}}`). Last-edit query uses scoped-then-windowed pattern (`Window(RowNumber(), partition_by=[object_id, field_name], order_by=created_at DESC)` → filter `rn=1` via `Subquery(values('pk'))`) to stay MSSQL-safe and bounded to visible shipments only.
  - **Field-history endpoint (D8 read path B).** `GET /api/v1/export/shipments/{id}/field-history/?field=<field_key>&limit=50` returns `{results: [{user_id, user_name, old_value, new_value, edited_at}, ...]}` newest-first. Permission: `can_edit_sheet_field` only (privacy — historical values may include old buyer prices etc.). Limit hard-capped at 200; default 50.
  - **Admin viewset (D5 + D6).** `backend/apps/export/views_sheet_settings.py` — `SheetRowSettingViewSet` at `admin/sheet-rows/`, `lookup_field='field_key'`. `list()` auto-creates missing rows from `DEFAULT_SHEET_ROWS` lazily on first GET. `perform_update` writes per-changed-field `AuditLog` rows for `triggered_role` and `triggered_user`. POST and DELETE disabled (settings registered, never created/deleted). XOR auto-clear: setting `triggered_role` clears `triggered_user`, vice versa; sending both non-empty in one PATCH → 400. Permission gate inherits sibling-tab pattern (`canDo(user, 'shipment', 'edit')`) — see "Known follow-ups" below.
  - **Frontend admin tab (D7).** `frontend/src/pages/admin/shipment-settings/SheetRowsTab.tsx` is now the fifth tab in `ShipmentSettingsPage`. Ant `Table` with `[#, Field, Default Who, Trigger Role, Trigger User]` columns; per-row save, toast on success. User picker carries the D3 tooltip ("For duties shared by multiple people, assign by role instead.") `triggered_user_active === false` renders an Ant warning chip — does not auto-clear, does not block save (D7 — visible-but-not-prescriptive). Hooks: `useSheetRowSettings`, `useSaveSheetRowSetting` (invalidates both `['admin', 'sheet-rows']` and `['shipments', 'sheet']` so the live Sheet reflects the change immediately).
  - **Sheet edit-gate + history marker.** `SheetLabelColumn` resolves `row_settings[field_key].triggered_label` (user name OR role label) before falling back to `t(default_who_key)`. `SheetCell` AND-composes `row_settings[field_key].can_current_user_edit` into the existing `canEditField` gate; locked cells show a tooltip explaining whether the lock is by role or by user. New `CellLastEditMarker` component (clock-icon badge harmonised with `CommentMarker`) appears on every cell present in `last_edits[shipment_id]`. Hover → tooltip with `"Last edited by {user} on {date} — {old} → {new}"`. Click → Ant `Popover` lazy-fetches `useFieldHistory` and lists prior edits newest-first; 403 from the endpoint surfaces as `t('sheet.history_forbidden')` ("No history available").
  - **i18n (all three locales).** New keys in tk/ru/en: `shipment_settings.tab_sheet_rows`, `sheet_rows.col_*`, `sheet_rows.locked_by_role` / `locked_by_user`, `sheet_rows.user_inactive_warning`, `sheet_rows.tooltip_user_picker`, `sheet_rows.toast_saved`, `sheet.last_edit_tooltip`, `sheet.history_title`, `sheet.history_empty`, `sheet.history_forbidden`. Plus `frontend/src/constants/roles.ts` mirroring backend `ROLE_CHOICES` for the role select.
  - **Tests.** 56 new tests across 4 files — `tests_sheet_perms.py` (17), `tests_shipment_field_audit.py` (8), `tests_sheet_settings_admin.py` (14), `tests_field_history.py` (12), plus `SheetExtendedResponseTests` (6 tests in `tests_shipment_sheet.py`). All pass under `USE_SQLITE=true`. Query-count regression guard on `/sheet/` enforced via bounded `CaptureQueriesContext` (7–12 queries; the bound tolerates `DynamicResourcePermission`'s warm-vs-cold cache jitter).
  - **Known follow-ups intentionally not addressed (filed as separate tickets):** (1) Admin tab gates — the sheet-rows tab inherits the existing `canDo(user, 'shipment', 'edit')` gate, which today resolves true for 8 of 13 roles; tightening this to director-only is its own ticket. (2) AuditLog retention/archive policy. (3) Audit `save()` side-effect field changes (computed totals, auto status mutations) — those need service-level hooks, separate from this hook which audits only what the user submitted.
- **PWA (installable + offline-shell)** (feat(frontend))
  - Added `vite-plugin-pwa@^0.21.2` (devDependency); registered via `registerType: 'autoUpdate'` + `injectRegister: 'auto'` so no manual `navigator.serviceWorker.register` call is needed
  - `vite.config.ts` now generates `manifest.webmanifest` (name "YGT Export Platform", short_name "YGT", `display: standalone`, theme `#1677ff`, background `#001529`) and references `/icon.svg` (`any`) + `/icon-maskable.svg` (`maskable`) — SVG-only, no binary PNGs
  - Workbox runtime caching: app shell (js/css/html/svg/woff2) precached; `/api/*` is `NetworkFirst` GET-only with `/api/v1/auth/*` excluded (httpOnly cookie security — never serve auth from cache); 100-entry / 5-minute cap; Google Fonts split into stylesheets (SWR) + webfonts (CacheFirst, 1y)
  - `index.html` adds `<link rel=apple-touch-icon>`, `<meta name=theme-color>`, description meta; old `/vite.svg` favicon replaced with `/icon.svg`
  - New `useOnlineStatus` hook (`navigator.onLine` + `online`/`offline` window events) and `<ConnectionStatus>` indicator (green/red dot with tooltip) wired into `AppLayout` header next to the language switcher
  - i18n: new `connection.online` / `connection.offline` keys in tk/ru/en
  - Scope: installable + app-shell cache + connection indicator. **Not** included: IndexedDB sync queue / offline mutations — that is a separate feature

### Added
- **Sheet Control v2 — Phase 1 backend** (feat(p3)) — complete replacement of the v1 JSONField/single-role model with a split-column + child-table design (ADR-0008/ADR-0009/ADR-0010). New features: `is_locked` BooleanField (explicit lock, replaces the "inactive user = lock" hack), `SheetRowRoleTrigger` child table (multiple roles per row), `SheetRowUserPermission` child table (per-user exceptions with soft-delete), sparse `display_order` (step 1024, ADR-0007), optimistic version field (409 on mismatch, ADR-0006), per-field localized `label_tk/ru/en` and `description_tk/ru/en` (split columns, not JSONField), `style_width/align/color` style columns, `deleted_at/deleted_by` soft-delete on both `SheetRowSetting` and `SheetRowUserPermission`.
  - **Migrations** (additive, ADR-0009): `0031` adds all v2 columns to `SheetRowSetting` + data steps (backfill `display_order = row_number * 1024`; migrate inactive-user rows to `is_locked=True`); `0032` creates `SheetRowRoleTrigger` + data step (copies `triggered_role` → child table) + removes `triggered_role` field (after constraint drop); `0033` creates `SheetRowUserPermission` with partial unique constraint (MSSQL filtered index).
  - **Permission helpers updated** (`apps/core/permissions.py`): `can_edit_sheet_field` and `get_sheet_edit_map` now check `role_triggers` (multi-role) and `user_permissions` (extra-user exceptions) using `prefetch_related`; query budget 4 (settings + 2 prefetch + field perms). Director/admin/superuser bypass unchanged.
  - **Admin viewset rewritten** (`views_sheet_settings.py`): `lookup_field='id'` (stable numeric id instead of field_key); `PATCH` with version check (409 on mismatch); `DELETE` soft-delete with hidden-≥30d pre-condition; `POST /restore/`; `POST /reorder/` (sparse display_order); `POST /permissions/bulk/` (grant/revoke user exceptions with idempotent get_or_create + soft-delete revoke). All mutations write per-field `AuditLog` rows.
  - **`/sheet/` payload v2**: `row_settings` now contains `labels`/`description`/`style` nested objects, `triggered_roles[]`, `extra_user_ids[]`, `is_locked`, `version`, `settings_updated_at/by_id`. Hidden rows (`is_visible=False`) excluded from `row_settings` entirely. New root keys: `users_index` (compact `{user_id: {name, role}}`), `current_user_id`, `current_user_lang`.
  - **Tests rewritten for v2**: `tests_sheet_perms.py` (29 tests covering role triggers, extra users, is_locked, soft-deleted grants, hidden rows, query count); `tests_sheet_settings_admin.py` (27 tests: version 409, sparse reorder, soft-delete pre-conditions, restore, permissions/bulk idempotent, AuditLog assertions); `tests_shipment_sheet.py` v2 keys + `users_index`/`current_user_lang` root fields + hidden-row exclusion test. All pass.
  - **Greenhouse services package** fixed: `submit_harvest_plan`, `approve_harvest_plan`, `reject_harvest_plan` migrated to `services/plan_workflow.py` and re-exported from `services/__init__.py` (blocked import error when `services/` package shadowed old `services.py`).

- **Sheet R15 (`vehicle_live_status`) and R20 (`loading_ended_at`)** (feat(p3)) — two formerly-orphan rows now have backing columns. R15 is a free-text field (max 300, Cyrillic_General_CI_AS) maintained by the dispatcher (`transport` role) describing where the truck is right now and ETA back to the greenhouse — distinct from R2 `route_note` (general transport note) and R3 `vehicle_condition` (mechanical state). R20 is an operator-entered datetime for when loading actually finished, distinct from R21 `departed_at` which is AD-1 and set by `transition_to('yola_chykdy', …)`. Migration `export.0025_shipment_live_status_loading_ended` adds both columns; both are added to `_ALL_PATCHABLE_FIELDS`, `ShipmentSheetSerializer.fields`, and `permission_registry.SHIPMENT_FIELDS`. `seed_permissions` grants `vehicle_live_status` to `transport` and `loading_ended_at` to `warehouse_chief`. R16 (`product_date`) was a placeholder with no backend and no domain meaning — removed from `SHEET_ROW_CONFIG` entirely. New i18n key `sheet.row.vehicle_live_status` in tk/ru/en

### Fixed
- **Sub-blocks (e.g. `OD`, `OG` under `O`) appeared as separate rows in the weekly harvest plan grid** (fix(p3)) — `GreenhouseBlock` is a self-referencing tree where parent blocks (`A`–`O`, `M5`, `M15`) are the unit of export/sales planning and sub-blocks exist only for internal block-level control. `initialize_harvest_week` in `apps/greenhouse/services.py` filtered `is_active=True` only, so it seeded `WeeklyHarvestPlan` rows for parents AND children, leaving the grid showing `O` plus `OD` plus `OG` (triple-counted in totals/truck-estimate). Filter is now `is_active=True, parent__isnull=True`. `WeeklyHarvestPlanViewSet.queryset` defensively adds `block__parent__isnull=True` so any pre-existing sub-block rows from past initializations stop reaching the API. Migration `greenhouse.0003_delete_subblock_harvest_plans` is a one-shot `RunPython` that deletes every `WeeklyHarvestPlan` whose block has a non-null parent (no-op reverse — the orphan rows had no business existing). Import command `import_harvest_plans` was already parent-only — its `BLOCK_NAME_PATTERNS` map never contained `OD`/`OG`, so the historical pollution came exclusively from `initialize_week` calls
- **`import_firm_name` always rendered as `—` even after a successful save** (fix(p3)) — `ShipmentSheetSerializer.import_firm_name` was sourced from `import_firm.name_en`, but `ImportFirm` has no `name_en` field (only `name_company` required, `name_short` optional). DRF silently returned `None` on every read, so the cell stayed blank no matter what the user picked even though the FK was being persisted correctly. Replaced with a `SerializerMethodField` that mirrors the model's `__str__` (`name_short or name_company`). All other FK display fields (country/customer/city/variety/border_point/export_firm) were verified — only import_firm had this latent bug
- **Sheet FK dropdown values flashed stale until refetch** (fix(p3)) — `import_firm`, `country`, `customer`, `city`, `variety`, `border_point` cells read `<field>_name`, but the optimistic update only wrote the FK id. After picking a new value the cell stayed on the old name (or `—`) for the duration of the round-trip. `useShipmentPatch` now accepts an `optimisticDisplay` map (cache-only, never sent over wire), and the dropdown editor passes the picked option's label so the matching `_name` updates instantly
- **Sheet rows that silently no-oped on save are now read-only** (fix(p3)) — two classes of broken rows: AD-1 timestamps (R19 `loading_started_at`, R21 `departed_at`, R25 `customs_exit_at`, R30 `border_crossed_at`, R32 `customs_entry_at`, R35 `arrived_at`, R41 `sale_started_at`, R42 `sale_ended_at`) which per ADR-010 are written ONLY by `transition_to()`; and orphan fields not present on the Shipment model / `IShipmentSheetItem` (R15 `truck_capacity`, R16 `product_date`, R20 `loading_ended_at`, R23 `truck_plate`, R26 `transit_days_temp`, R27 `driver_name`, R28 `driver_phone`, R31 `dest_entry_at`, R39 `harvest_date`, R44 `additional_notes_arap`) where the PATCH was returning 200 with the field silently stripped by `ShipmentPatchSerializer`. All marked `inputType: 'readonly'` until either the backend column is added (orphans) or a status transition is the right path (AD-1)
- **Sheet multi-select editor closed after the first pick** (fix(p3)) — `block_sources` (R8) and `firm_splits` (R9) called `saveJunction` on every `onChange`. Its `onSuccess` invalidates the sheet query and calls `close()`, which sets `editingCell=null` and unmounts the editor — so the user could never select a second block/firm. Editor now buffers selections in a ref and commits a single junction save when the dropdown closes (or the cell loses focus). A no-op selection (same set of ids) just closes without an HTTP round trip
- **Sheet inline edits silently failed on every cell** (fix(p3)) — `useShipmentSheet` was changed in 832ad14 to cache `{shipments, comment_counts, task_counts}` (object) instead of a flat array, but `useShipmentPatch`'s optimistic update still called `old.map(...)` on the cache value. The `TypeError` thrown synchronously inside `onMutate` aborted the mutation before TanStack Query fired the PATCH request, so no value persisted and the user just saw the generic save-error toast. Patch now updates `old.shipments` while preserving the `comment_counts` / `task_counts` siblings, and the context type is corrected to `IShipmentSheetResult` (now exported from `useShipmentSheet`). Affected every editable Sheet cell, not only rows 2–6 — that range is just where the user happened to test first
- **Page-level double-scroll & hidden horizontal scrollbar across all pages** (fix(frontend))
  - `AppLayout.tsx` `<Content>` is now the scroll boundary (`height: calc(100vh - 56px)`, `overflowY: auto`, `overflowX: hidden`); browser window/body never scrolls again
  - Generalized the ShipmentSheet `:has(> .sheet-page)` rule in `SheetStyles.css` to a reusable `.page-fullheight-grid` class for any page that wants to fill the Content area and own its own scrolling
  - Added `scroll={{ x: 'max-content' }}` to every wide Ant Design `<Table>` / `<ProTable>` so each table has its own horizontal scrollbar at its bottom edge instead of the document bottom — visible immediately when columns overflow. Affected pages: ShipmentList, ShipmentDetail (firm splits), ExportFirms, ImportFirms, Customers, Blocks, BlockDetail, TruckDestinations, BorderPointsTab, TruckSplitsTab, OptionListsTab, StatusesTab, QuotaPerFirmTable, QuotaWeeklyFlow (outer + inner), boss/TopCustomers, boss/RoutePnlTable, boss/FirmRiskMatrix
  - Boss dashboard tables that already had `scroll={{ y: ... }}` now merge `x: 'max-content'` so both axes work

### Added
- **Shipments page — web-management upgrade** (feat(p3))
  - **Detail page edit drawers** — every section on `ShipmentDetail` (Logistics / Transport / Goods / Finance / Status / Notes) now exposes a per-section "Edit" button that opens an Ant Design `Drawer` with a `Form`. Permission-gated per field via `canEditField`. The previously-disabled top-right "Edit" button now opens the drawer with all groups
  - **List row Edit drawer** — new `ShipmentEditDrawerForId` wrapper fetches `useShipmentDetail` on demand. The List has an "Edit" action button per row that opens the same drawer used on Detail
  - **Bulk row select + bulk transition** — ProTable `rowSelection` enabled. New `ShipmentBulkTransitionModal` fans out `POST /shipments/{id}/transition/` per selected id (no new backend endpoint), shows `Progress` bar, partial-success summary with per-row error list
  - **Advanced filter drawer** — new `ShipmentFilterDrawer` slides in from the right with country, customer, export firm, date range, and "pending my fields" toggle. Reflects to URL via `useSearchParams` (`country`, `customer`, `export_firm`, `date_after`, `date_before`, `pending_my_fields`); active filters render as closeable `Tag` chips
  - **Backend filter extensions** — `ShipmentViewSet` now accepts `customer`, `export_firm` (via `firm_splits__export_firm` with `Exists()` to avoid join duplicates), `date_after`, `date_before` query params on `GET /api/v1/export/shipments/`
  - **Saved column visibility + reorder** — ProTable `columnsState={{ persistenceKey: 'shipment-list-columns', persistenceType: 'localStorage' }}` plus stable `key` on every column. Settings cog appears in the toolbar via `options.setting`
  - **Card / Table view toggle** — `Segmented` switch reflected in URL as `?display=cards`. New AntD-based `ShipmentCardAnt` (separate from the Mantine Kanban card) renders a CSS-grid of cards with the same pagination
  - **Reusable building blocks** — `FieldEditor` shared input switcher (text / textarea / number / date / datetime / select / option_select / boolean), `EDIT_FIELD_GROUPS` config (`shipmentEditConfig.ts`) as the single source of truth for which fields belong to which group and what input type each one needs, and `useShipmentPatchMulti` hook (multi-field PATCH with the same optimistic-update strategy as the single-field hook)
  - **Detail serializer FK ids** — `ShipmentDetailSerializer` now exposes `country`, `customer`, `city`, `variety`, `border_point`, `import_firm`, `loading_location` ids (plus `rejected_weight_kg`, `vehicle_responsible`) so dropdowns in the drawer can preselect without an extra resolution call
  - **i18n** — `shipment_edit_drawer.*`, `shipment_bulk.*`, `shipment_filter_drawer.*`, `shipment_list.view_table` / `view_cards`, `shipments.empty_state` added in tk/ru/en simultaneously per the strict i18n rule

### Fixed
- **Inline edit on List date columns silently no-oped** — earlier inline cell edit had `departed_at` and `arrived_at` editable, but those are AD-1 timestamps that `ShipmentPatchSerializer` excludes from `_ALL_PATCHABLE_FIELDS`, so the PATCH body was silently dropped server-side and the value reverted on refetch. Removed inline edit on those two columns; only `weight_net` remains inline-editable on the List (matches what's actually patchable). Datetimes are still editable in the per-section Edit drawer when the field is in the patchable set (fix(p3))

### Added
- **Truck split defaults — admin-configurable official kg-per-firm table** (Gap 7, ADR-016) (feat(p3))
  - New `TruckSplitDefault` model (`backend/apps/export/models/quota.py`) keyed by `num_firms`; migration `0023_truck_split_defaults` seeds (1 → 18,100), (2 → 9,000), (3 → 6,000)
  - Follow-up migration `0024_truck_split_notes_cyrillic` applies `COLLATE Cyrillic_General_CI_AS` to `notes` via raw SQL (mssql-django doesn't emit collation-only `ALTER COLUMN`); SQLite-guarded so dev shim still works
  - DDL reference (`database/ygt_platform_ddl_v5_1.sql`) extended with `export.truck_split_defaults` table definition
  - `get_default_truck_weight(N)` now reads from the DB with a 5-min cache; `invalidate_truck_split_cache()` helper
  - Director-only CRUD endpoints `/api/v1/export/admin/truck-splits/` (`TruckSplitDefaultViewSet` + `TruckSplitDefaultSerializer`); new `truck_split_default` resource registered in `permission_registry.py` and seeded so director gets full CRUD, export_manager read-only
  - New "Truck Split Defaults" tab on `/admin/shipment-settings` (`TruckSplitsTab.tsx`); hooks `useTruckSplits`, `useCreateTruckSplit`, `useUpdateTruckSplit`, `useDeleteTruckSplit` in `useAdmin.ts`
  - i18n: new `truck_split.*` namespace + `shipment_settings.tab_truck_splits` in tk/ru/en
  - 6 new sheet tests + 10 admin CRUD tests (`tests_truck_split_admin.py`); covers auto-fill, fallback, explicit override, cache invalidation, role gates, validation
  - ADR-016 documents the official-vs-real distinction so future contributors don't try to "fix" the gap between `Sum(firm_splits.weight_kg) ≈ 18,100` and the real `Shipment.weight_net ≈ 18,500–21,000` — that gap is intentional

### Fixed
- **Sheet R8/R9 multiselect saved `weight_kg = 0`** (Gap 7) — selecting blocks/firms in the multiselect cells now persists correct weights (feat(p3))
  - Frontend (`SheetCellEditor.tsx`) drops the hardcoded `weight_kg: 0` literal; sends only IDs
  - Backend `set_block_sources` auto-fills with `(weight_net or 18,100) / N`, last entry takes the rounding remainder
  - Backend `set_firm_splits` auto-fills with the OFFICIAL `TruckSplitDefault[N]` value (capped at 18,100 — see ADR-016)
  - Explicit non-zero `weight_kg` from the client is honoured (admin override path)
  - Updated Obsidian doc `screens/shipment-sheet.md` with the new auto-split behaviour and link to the admin tab

### Added
- **Configurable freeze panes on the Shipment Sheet** — Google Sheets-style freeze for both rows and columns (feat(frontend))
  - `Freeze` dropdown in `SheetToolbar` with row/column options (No, 1, 2, Up to current, Default 13 rows)
  - State in `sheetStore` (`frozenRowCount`, `frozenColCount`) persisted to `localStorage` under `ygt-sheet-freeze`
  - Frozen data columns rendered as `position: sticky; left: <offset>` cells between the label band and the virtualizer; remaining shipments are passed to `@tanstack/react-virtual`
  - Header row's left labels (`#`/Who/Field name) are now sticky-left so they stay visible during horizontal scrolling — fixes a long-standing bug where they would scroll out of view
  - Blue 2px line on the trailing edge of the last frozen row/column marks the freeze boundary; freeze counts clamp against visible rows/columns each render so stale localStorage values still produce a coherent layout
  - i18n: 14 new `sheet.freeze.*` keys added to all three locales

- **Comment + task system (backend)** — cell-anchored threaded comments with @user / @role mentions and single-assignee task assignment on `export.shipment_comments`. New fields: `field_key`, `role_mentions`, `assignee`, `is_done`, `done_at`, `done_by`, `is_deleted`. Indexes: `ix_comments_shipment_field`, `ix_comments_assignee_open`. Migration `0021_comment_cells_tasks` (feat(p3))
- `Notification.KIND_CHOICES` extended with `mention`, `task_assigned`, `task_done` (feat(p3))
- `apps/export/services/comments.py` — `create_comment()`, `mark_task_done()`, `reopen_task()`, `_fan_out_notifications()` service layer; all mutations wrapped in `transaction.atomic()`, bulk_create with `batch_size=500` (feat(p3))
- `CommentViewSet` at `GET/POST /api/v1/export/comments/` + `PATCH/DELETE /{id}/` + `POST /{id}/done/` + `POST /{id}/reopen/`. Filters: `shipment`, `field_key`, `assignee` (accepts `me`), `is_done`, `parent_comment=null`. Soft-delete via `is_deleted=True` (feat(p3))
- `CommentSerializer` (read) extended with `field_key`, `assignee`, `assignee_name`, `is_done`, `done_at`, `done_by_name`, `mentions_ids`, `role_mentions_list`, `replies_count`, `is_deleted`. `CommentCreateSerializer` (write) with int ID fields to avoid DRF PrimaryKeyRelatedField queryset=None issue on MSSQL (feat(p3))
- `GET /api/v1/export/shipments/sheet/` response now wraps `results` array with `comment_counts` and `task_counts` top-level dicts (keyed by shipment_id) — three extra queries, no N+1 (feat(p3))
- `GET /api/v1/core/users/mentionable/?q=&limit=10` — mixed users+roles autocomplete endpoint for the @mention popover (feat(p3))
- `MentionUserSerializer`, `MentionRoleSerializer` serializer classes (feat(p3))
- `SHEET_FIELD_KEYS` frozenset in `services/comments.py` (42 keys, derived from `sheetRowConfig.ts`) — field_key allowlist validation (feat(p3))
- 11 backend tests in `apps/export/tests_comments.py` covering: user mention creates notification, role mention deduplication, assignee gets task_assigned only, reply inherits field_key, task done idempotent, bulk_create batch_size check, legacy endpoint backward compat (test(p3))
- `IShipmentSheetResponse`, `ISheetCommentCounts`, `ISheetTaskCounts` TypeScript interfaces in `frontend/src/types/index.ts` (feat(frontend))
- **Comment + task system (frontend)** — right-side `CommentsDrawer` on `/export/shipments/sheet` (`mask=false`, 360px) with filter chips (This cell / All cells / My tasks), composer with `@`/`#` mention popovers, cell-anchor toggle, and single-assignee task picker (feat(frontend))
- New components under `frontend/src/components/sheet/`: `CommentsDrawer`, `CommentList`, `CommentItem`, `CommentComposer`, `MentionPopover`, `CommentMarker` — no mention library, custom popover in ~80 lines per `mssql-compat.md` "no heavy deps" stance (feat(frontend))
